Hello,
While visiting my favourite American-style diner this week, I noticed that the ranch salad dressing wasn't Medley Ranch Dressing anymore. The server explained that your company had recently decided to stop selling it in Singapore.
This was quite disappointing to hear, as I have been a fan of Medley Ranch Dressing since I studied abroad in the USA twenty years ago. I stayed with an American family that always used to have that big, round bottle on the dinner table, and now the tangy, creamy taste brings up good memories of that period for me.
So I am wondering—would it be possible to buy a few leftover bottles of the dressing, if you have them? I don't mind buying a case, because I remember that it has a long shelf life. Please let me know.
Sincerely,
Lynette Chua
From:customerservice@medleysg.sg
To:lynettechua@tilmail.com
Subject:Re: Medley Ranch Dressing
Date:4 December
Attachment:Order_form
Dear Ms. Chua,
Thank you for contacting Medley Singapore. I'm pleased to inform you that we do have a small quantity of Medley Ranch Dressing left. I have attached a form you can use to order some.
We were sorry to discontinue the product in Singapore. We had hoped that the Medley Ranch Dressing in its slim, sleek bottle would appeal to Singaporeans' cosmopolitan tastes. However, there just isn't much demand for creamy salad dressings here, and we were unsuccessful in convincing people to use the product in marinades, dips, and sandwiches like our American customers do. Our management had to make the smart business decision.
We do still offer one creamy dressing, and I'd be happy to send you a sample of it as a token of our appreciation for your loyalty to the Medley brand. If you're interested, please reply with your mailing address.
Sincerely,
Justin Tan

Instead, its sixth season will be shot at Hobson's Studios in Melbourne, far from the network's Sydney headquarters. "We looked at facilities in Brisbane and Perth as well, but only Hobson has a soundstage with the capacity to comfortably hold our live audience," explained Lauren Campbell, ABS's head of production. Anticipation is high for Dance for the Gold's sixth season after its highly rated fifth season, even though popular judge Santino Nucci will not be returning. Based on an idea attributed to Ms. Campbell, the program features aspiring professional dancers who compete to master a variety of dance styles for the approval of expert judges. Tech Helpers
Galindo Public Library’s collection now includes a wealth of digital content, from e-books to streaming films. We are recruiting volunteer “Tech Helpers” to assist patrons with accessing our electronic resources, in addition to teaching basic tech skills such as sending e-mail.
Requirements:
- Considerable proficiency with computers, mobile devices, and the Internet\n- Positive demeanor and the ability to explain concepts clearly and patiently\n- Minimum commitment of two hours per week for six months
To express interest in becoming a Tech Helper, send an e-mail to volunteer@ galindopubliclibrary.org.

(A) Submit comments before a certain date (B) Check a map showing roadwork sites (C) Take note of temporary closures (D) Plan journeys with the help of a mobile app # City Approves Transformation of Pinebrook Mall Site Oct. 11—The city of Hawthorn has approved a proposal to redevelop Pinebrook Mall, which has sat empty for the past year. After demolishing most of the structure and its parking area, developer Austin Partners plans to build a large apartment complex bordered by an office building, a few new storefronts, and a community park with a playground and sports fields. Austin Partners conceived the project after the recent relaxation of Hawthorn's zoning laws to allow some mixed-use developments in commercial zones—a change made to help the city meet increasing demand for housing. The influx of people hired by Hawthorn's booming medical technology firms has put a strain on its existing supply. “Hawthorn doesn’t have much completely undeveloped land anymore, but it does have a few underused retail complexes,” said Geeta Nathwani, Austin Partners’ vice president of development. “It’s a reflection of the rise of online shopping. We hope that the successful redevelopment of Pinebrook Mall will lead to similar projects by demonstrating the potential of this approach.” 155. PHILADELPHIA (April 7)—Fast food chain Zora's announced yesterday that it will begin rolling out an artificial intelligence (AI) chatbot to take orders for its drive-through service in June.—[1]— Company officials explained that drive-through customers who pull up to one of the chain's new ordering stations will be able to speak their order out loud or ask questions about the menu just as they could to a human worker. — [2] —. The technology will handle the interaction and transmit the order to the restaurant's staff for preparation. “This project came out of a desire to reduce waiting times for our drive-through customers by speeding up the ordering process,” said Zora’s CEO Curtis Hendricks. The chatbot will be powered by software developed by Innovix that can recognize the meaning of speech and generate appropriate responses in natural language. Zora's software engineers have been working with Innovix to customize the chatbot for its task. ——[3]——. “The team has put a lot of effort into avoiding potential pitfalls,” said Mr. Hendricks. “For example, they’ve taught the chatbot all of the substitute terms that people like to use when ordering.” He cited customers’ habits of calling the chain’s Big Bacon Cheeseburger a “BBC” and using the generic term “milkshake” for its Chiller desserts. — [4] —. Despite such reassurances, the news has received a largely negative response from the public. Many social media users expressed suspicion that the move was actually a way for Zora's, the fifth-largest restaurant chain in the U.S., to save money on personnel costs. 168. The Dornsby Growers Alliance will begin including live musical performances at our weekly farmers market to create a more festive atmosphere for shoppers. Visit our Web site to apply! http://www.b2breviews.com/packaging Customer Review: Merchandise Bags Review posted by: Silvia Gordon I run a small health food store and have usually stocked transparent Gaines bags to give to customers who need to carry merchandise home. These bags are made of recycled plastic but can't be recycled themselves. So, I was excited to hear about Lozano through a retail newsletter. (A) A real estate agency (B) A cleaning company (C) An interior design firm (D) A moving service # ～ # Invitation to Bid ##### Purpose The City of Rowland is seeking bids for the provision of solar-powered waste compacting bins and accompanying software. Rowland intends to modernize the sanitation system in its downtown area. ## Background The metal trash and recycling bins that the city currently offers for public use downtown often overflow, and municipal workers must check their status in person. In addition to compressing waste so that more can be added, solar-powered waste compacting bins are smart devices that monitor and report their fullness levels via built-in Wi-Fi. Cities across the country have increased the efficiency of their sanitation departments by adopting them. Studies also show that the installation of solar-powered waste compacting bins has reduced the amount of litter on these municipalities' sidewalks and parkland. ##### Project Specifications The selected firm will supply Rowland with 70 solar-powered waste compacting bins for trash and recycling collection plus a software program to oversee them. Each bin must be able to hold at least 130 gallons of waste and include a foot pedal for hands-free opening. The bins must have a backup battery and a digital display that will clearly communicate fullness level to passers-by. The bins must be leak-proof and inaccessible to most pests. Please submit a bid with detailed information about costs and timelines by 6 P.M. on September 4 to the City of Rowland Procurement Division. 164. What is the purpose of the information? # Improving the Copywriting for Watchling Videos By Hyun-Mi Chang The video sharing platform Watchling is a great promotional tool for individuals and businesses. However, as both a video consumer and a marketing professional, I sometimes find myself frustrated with the copy that Watchling's creators write for their video titles and descriptions. Too many titles are confusingly worded or misleading about the video's content. Many long descriptions place important links to the creator's Web site or video channel at the end, where they are not visible to viewers who don't click "Show More." Some creators may have the ability to improve their copywriting on their own. However, Watchling should also take some steps to assist video creators, who are the backbone of its business model. Free workshops on copywriting, as well as other aspects of the video production process, would be wise. The platform's technical team should consider how adjusting its algorithm could resolve some of these issues, too. 176. How does Mr. Hayes want Ms. Knapp to revise a template? (A) By reducing the level of detail in a description (B) By featuring some information near the top (C) By using certain keywords associated with a topic (D) By displaying Web links in a more eye-catching color 177. Owned by industry legend Yesmina Davids, this 42-seat café and roastery in the Central Business District has a cozy atmosphere, with plush furniture and vintage-themed décor. The friendly counter staff is knowledgeable about the menu, which spans typical items like café mochas and café lattes as well as unique offerings like a "sunrise espresso" made with orange juice. I've enjoyed everything I've tried, though in my opinion, nothing beats a simple espresso made from the café's signature CT Blend. I recommend pairing it with their buttery, flaky croissant. Dog owners will be happy to know that the café welcomes their furry friends on weekly Dog-Friendly Sundays. —Charles, 14 August https://www.everydayreviews.co.za/ctcoffeeworks ##### CT Coffeeworks: 3/5 stars I'm an avid reader of The Coffee Guy, Charles Maseko's blog, and so I decided to visit CT Coffeeworks after reading his recent post about it. I arrived with a good book just after opening time yesterday, but ultimately found it difficult to concentrate on reading after some patrons brought in their energetic dogs. As for the food, I ordered an espresso and an apple tart, which looked very appetising in the display case. The espresso was rich and full-bodied with hints of chocolate, but the apple tart turned out to be bland. When I returned my dishes to the counter, the pastry chef did notice that I hadn't finished it and apologised after I explained why. All in all, it was a mixed experience and I'm not sure I'll go there again. Posted on 20 August by Trent Morrow (trentmorrow@go-mail.co.za)

Ms. Zhang has accepted an appointment to the National Resources Conservation Agency, where she will administer public grants for projects that promote innovation in conservation. The BFSI is the country's largest nonprofit organization dedicated to promoting efficient irrigation practices in agriculture. In addition to providing direct support to small-scale farms to improve their watering systems, its activities include research, education, and advocacy. It is now seeking an experienced professional to replace Ms. Zhang. The ideal candidate will have a background in the field, strong problem-solving and communication skills, and enthusiasm for implementing the executive director's vision. Those interested in the position should visit bfsi.org to learn more.

##### https://www.aloftairlines.com/refunds #### Voluntary Cancellation and Refund Policy Tickets purchased directly from Aloft Airlines can be canceled through our Web site or reservation phone line. To request a cancellation, $ \underline{\text{log in}} $ to your Aloft Airlines account online and open "My Trips," or call 1-800-555-0129 and select the appropriate option from the menu. For reservations made through a travel agent, please contact the travel agent. Cancellations made within 24 hours of the ticket purchase qualify the customer for a refund of all fees except the booking fee. After that point, refund eligibility depends on the type of fare purchased. Check your ticket receipt to see the fare rules. Refunds are credited back to the original form of payment. Please allow up to 7 business days for processing. Involuntary cancellations (those initiated by Aloft Airlines) are subject to different rules. Built by local developer Leonard Moore over 40 years ago, Mooretown Mall is the oldest continuously operating shopping center in Shannon County. — [1] —. It is owned and managed with care by Mr. Moore's descendants through Moore Enterprises. Spearsley's top shopping and dining destination in its early decades, the mall overcame the closure of many of its tenants during the rise of online shopping. — [2] —. Now occupied by a vibrant variety of businesses, Mooretown Mall often attracts shoppers, diners, and visitors from as far away as Wenglade and Bryantburg. On its east side, Mooretown Mall is anchored by the largest Clurett Department Store in Greater Ingramville, situated in a striking section that retains the building's original post-modern architecture. Within the mall's west side lie Well Foods supermarket and a five-screen King Cinema, while smaller boutique shops in between specialize in everything from jewelry to video games. Restaurants include Flores Mexican Grill, Harper Steakhouse, and Elena's, and patrons seeking lighter fare can stop at Jaybird Juice or Alden Pretzels. — [3] —. Beyond retail and dining options, Mooretown Mall businesses offer services ranging from yoga classes to eye exams to auto maintenance. With wheelchair-accessible design, Mooretown Mall is welcoming to people of all physical abilities. — [4] —. Its elaborate seasonal decorations also make it a must-visit destination on major holidays. Proud of its history as a gathering place for several generations of Shannon County residents, Mooretown Mall continues to strive to provide a premier shopping experience.172. Summary: Data discovery software not neededUrgency: Low ✓ Medium High ___
Details:My work in the textbook development department involves writing content that features imagined characters. The new data discovery program keeps mistaking these characters' names and other information for "sensitive data" and requires me to explain why my writing files don't need security protections. I don't handle any real people's personal information, so the program is unnecessary for me. I would like IT to uninstall it from my computer or at least lower the scanning frequency.
Technician Assigned: Jazmin OrtegaDate Due: August 31
To:Edward Freeman
From:Jazmin Ortega
Subject:IT Work Order Request 8322
Date:August 31
Dear Mr. Freeman, I have been assigned to assist you with the work order you submitted yesterday regarding Decipher, our data discovery software. I understand your frustration with the program, but the recent tightening of laws around data protection has made this area a priority for the company. Employees are not allowed to remove Decipher completely or reduce the frequency of the scans. However, I can show you how to exempt a folder from scanning. If you save your writing files to that folder, you should no longer be inconvenienced by Decipher. I want to emphasize to you that this approach puts you at risk of violating our policy regarding the management of real people’s sensitive data should you come into possession of some in the future. This would have serious consequences for your employment here. To reduce this risk, you should not extend exempt status to other folders or use your exempt folder to store all your files. If you can agree to these terms, I will be happy to visit your workstation at your convenience. Jazmin Ortega Information Technology Specialist

We melt a special mix of iron and other ingredients. 2. We cast the cookware in traditional sand molds. 3. We put the cooled pieces in a machine that uses tiny steel balls to clean off the sand. 4. We use hand tools to carefully remove any imperfections from the cookware's surface. 5. Depending on the product, we may also apply beautiful enamel or season the cookware with all-natural oils. ("Seasoning", which involves applying and heating an oil coating, protects cast iron from rusting.) (A) Love, Amy (B) Improv Madness (C) Hear Us Out (D) My Worst Job#### Instructions for Accessing Special Collections The Vanell University Library's special collections may be viewed only in the Special Collections Reading Room. Those interested in accessing materials in a special collection must follow these steps. 1. Create an account on the special collections Web site. 2. Use the site's search or browsing functions to find materials you wish to view. Check the box next to each and select "Request." 3. Schedule your viewing appointment on the calendar page. To allow time for staff to retrieve your desired materials, you will only be able to select time slots at least two business days in the future. 4. Submit your request. Submitted requests will be viewable and editable on your account page. Before attending your appointment, confirm that your request has been fulfilled, as requests for restricted materials may take longer to process. 5. Review the reading room guidelines to prepare for your visit and learn what items are prohibited.

GOODWIN (June 7)—The Goodwin Arts Council has announced the details of its plans to commemorate the 100th anniversary of Emory Shaffer's birth. The “Emory Shaffer Festival” will take place in the Curry Cultural Center on Saturday, July 24. The bestselling author of such classic children's books as Adeline and Jeffrey and the Sugar Garden was born in Goodwin, though he resided elsewhere as an adult. The festival will include lively readings of Mr. Shaffer's works and free screenings of their film adaptations, as well as child-friendly craft activities based on the happenings and characters in his stories. In the evening, local actors will stage scenes from the books in the center's auditorium. Goodwin Arts Council president Soo-Ae Gwon said the festival's organizers hope it captures the author's spirit. "Mr. Shaffer loved to have fun with language, using unusual words and even inventing his own, and that sense of whimsy is what we want to convey to attendees," she said. She promised that the festival would both provide a place for longtime fans to celebrate Mr. Shaffer and encourage young generations' interest in his time-honored stories. The Goodwin Arts Council is a nonprofit organization that also serves as the local arts agency for the City of Goodwin. 155. What is the purpose of the article? (A) To publicize a celebration for a popular author (B) To announce the opening of a cultural center (C) To promote the reissuing of some classic books (D) To introduce the new leader of an arts organization 156. What is planned for the evening of July 24? (A) An outdoor craft fair (B) An appearance by a celebrity (C) A screening of a documentary (D) A live theater performance 157. What does Ms. Gwon emphasize about Mr. Shaffer? (A) His contributions to Goodwin (B) His playful writing style (C) His enthusiasm for a variety of arts (D) His responsiveness to fans
##### https://www.crawford.com/about # About Crawford Solutions We are a locally-owned business dedicated to designing, printing, and applying eye-catching vinyl wraps and decals for vehicles. A wrap or decal featuring a business's name and logo effectively creates a mobile billboard out of any vehicle, from a compact car to a moving van. We use high-quality cast vinyl and do the printing in-house on a sophisticated large-format inkjet printer. The heart of Crawford Solutions is our staff, who handle every step of the process with passion and expertise. Our design team will find the best way to showcase your existing branding elements—or make striking graphics from scratch. For example, we recently came up with distinctive construction imagery for full-vehicle wraps for Kline Builders. The company has seen a significant increase in inquiries from potential customers who have seen its colorful trucks on the road and at job sites. The Timely 3, which will feature a three-in-one BioTrack health sensor and a Niqua Artisan operating system, will go on sale on December 4. The Timely 3 will offer a new "Tactile" model with a rotatable bezel (face frame) to control the watch in addition to the standard touch screen and side buttons. — [1] —. The Timely 3 Tactile will have steep price tags of $399 with Bluetooth connectivity or $349 with LTE connectivity. Fortunately, the Timely 3 Standard's Bluetooth and LTE types will cost just $299 and $249, respectively. Maddox product manager Evelyn Baker said these features were the result of feedback from Timely 2 users.—[2]—. “The main improvements that customers have requested are more health tracking capabilities and the option to have a bezel controller,” said Ms. Baker. “We are excited to be presenting a device that fulfills these criteria and also has increased access to apps thanks to the Niqua operating system.—[3]—.” Ms. Baker also emphasized a convenient similarity between the Timely 2 and the Timely 3. “We designed the watch band connecting points so that customers who upgrade can put their existing bands on their new device.—[4]—. We wanted to enable those who have invested in custom accessories to continue to use them.”172. What is the main purpose of the article? 1. Prepare bread\nUse a sourdough roll. For a medium sandwich, select a 6-inch roll. For a large sandwich, select a 10-inch roll. Slice the bread in half lengthwise.
2. Add meat\nUse sliced roast turkey. Add 3 slices for medium sandwiches and 5 slices for large sandwiches. If the customer requested a deluxe version, add Swiss cheese and bacon.
3. Toast\nPlace in a tray on the toaster. Press the button for the appropriate roll size.
4. Add vegetables\nUse 1 handful of lettuce and 2 tomatoes for a medium sandwich and 2 handfuls of lettuce and 3 tomatoes for a large sandwich. For deluxe sandwiches, spread mashed avocado on the top half of bread.
5. Add sauce\nChoose the Delight sauce bottle. Drizzle 2 lines of sauce along the vegetables.
6. Finish\nCut large sandwiches in half. Place the sandwich on wax paper for serving. For takeout orders, use tape to seal the paper, then write the sandwich's name on top.

##### National Postal Service Clarkton Branch Notice to Employees Shifts in the geographical distribution of Clarkton's population have altered the city's postal delivery service needs. Accordingly, we will be adjusting our delivery routes as described below, effective September 1. Mail carriers will receive information about route reassignments soon. Route A will continue to serve central and northern Clarkton. Its western boundaries will remain Haynes Street and Tate Avenue, but its main eastern boundary will be shifted one block to Park Street. Route B will now serve all addresses east of Park Street, which will greatly expand its southern area. It will also encompass Galindo Estates. Route C will begin serving a reduced area of southern Clarkton between Park Street and Delaney Road and will no longer include Galindo Estates and Whitaker Office Park. These changes will make its mail volume roughly similar to those of the other routes. Route D will continue to serve northwestern Clarkton from Haynes Street and Tate Avenue, but also extend southward and eastward all the way to Delaney Road and Whitaker Office Park.

The Murray Museum of Science & Technology will be holding a series of talks on the theme of science in everyday life. We have invited speakers with expertise in a range of subjects. Have you ever sat in an airplane and wondered how such a heavy machine is able to cruise through the clouds? Sophia Reilly, the museum's newest senior program manager, will give a talk on the science of flight. No matter the field, attendees will learn how earlier researchers and theorists made its major advancements and gain an understanding of the ideas underlying those advancements. Many will even leave with some valuable health advice or tips relating to their favorite hobbies. The talks will take place once or twice a month on Wednesdays, beginning February 26. Admission is free for museum members and $7 for non-members. Visit www.mmst.org/est to register. #### Everyday Science Series – Schedule ##### February 26 Taking to the Sky – Learn about the physics principles that allow us to travel by air from a former employee of the National Aerospace Research Agency (NARA). ##### March 26 Our Invisible Companions – Microscopic germs are everywhere. Discover how they cause disease and how good hygiene can protect our community. ##### April 16 Body Fuel – Make healthy choices for your body by learning how to apply the science of nutrition to your diet. ##### May 7 Growing Science – Even experienced gardeners will find useful information in this overview of research on plants, soil, and pests. ##### May 28 A Taste of Science – Improve your skills in the kitchen with insights from physics, chemistry, and biology. ##### June 18 Rain or Shine – Attendees will find out how temperature, air pressure, wind, and other factors determine our weather.

#### Review of Funworld's Eagle's Adventure By Hwa-Young Im, May 25 The newest addition to Funworld's Animal Kingdom section is a thrilling experience for children and adults alike. Though motion simulator rides are unpopular with some guests, Eagle's Adventure thankfully avoids the common pitfall of the type. No one in my party suffered the motion sickness that such rides often cause. Instead, everyone enjoyed the chance to experience the world from an eagle's perspective. Seated in a cart shaped like the bird, Eagle's Adventure riders swoop and soar together through forests, past waterfalls, and over other animals. The graphics are beautifully rendered on the enormous screen, and there are exciting water and lighting effects. The attraction also improves upon its predecessor, Ocean Journey, by treating those waiting in line to videos of eagles, photo opportunities with eagle statues, and chances to touch real eagle feathers. Eagle's Adventure is already open at Funworld Moorland and will launch at the Funworld park in Larkhaven on June 4. Guests must be at least 40 inches tall to board the ride.176. (A) On January 12 (B) On February 3 (C) On May 25 (D) On June 4Questions 181-185 refer to the following article and survey. ##### Elvet Now Home to Oswald Cinema DURHAM (4 May)—Those driving along Hollingside Lane may be surprised to see a replica of the city's long-gone Oswald Cinema standing on the other side of the hedge. The stunning brick building is the centrepiece of Elvet Museum's new 1950s-themed section. Museum director Ah-Yoon Ryu hosted local officials yesterday for the section's ribbon-cutting ceremony. The sprawling “living museum” complex now features recreations of four periods in the history of Northern England, starting with the 1820s. Each section is staffed with a cast of actors trained to play roles ranging from miners to nurses. The new Oswald is a functional cinema, with more than 200 seats. It screens classics by Preston Gilbert and other big names of 1950s cinema, as well as period newsreels and adverts. Souvenir tickets and tours of the projection booth are also available. Other highlights of Elvet's newest section include recreations of an electrical shop, a typical family home, and a community centre. Visitors can even refresh themselves at a café selling real traditional treats and playing popular music from the 1950s. For passes or more information, visit www.elvetmuseum.org.uk. Discussion: The average ratings displayed above were generated from 72 submitted surveys. The respondents' ratings and accompanying comments suggest some steps Iris Garden Center could take to improve customers' experiences. First, customers reported some trouble locating products in the center's outdoor spaces, mainly because of a lack of signage. Acumen recommends adding standing signs where possible and floor plans at the entrances. Customers were pleased with the staff's helpfulness but would prefer not to rely on them for finding their way. Another type of feedback found in several responses related to product selection. Customers are satisfied with your inventory of basic items—plants and gardening equipment. (D) She presented a purchase receipt to the cashier.BLACKWELL (September 5)—Diana Hobbs' first film, The New Sound, premiered at the Blackwell International Film Festival today. The accomplished singer-songwriter has made a highly watchable documentary on the so-called “Owensby sound,” a genre of country music most popular in the 1960s. The Owensby sound was characterized by fast tempos and electric instruments instead of the slower, orchestral tunes that were being produced in Hardin, the center of country music at the time. Ms. Hobbs' film traces the style's development and popularization by Owensby-based performers like Sadie Lowery and Boone Sheppard. She includes album clips and some archival video footage of their entertaining live performances, which often featured outlandish costumes. The highlight of the film, however, is her interviews with Mr. Sheppard, whom she knows from her childhood in Galstead, where Mr. Sheppard retired. They have a charming rapport, and he has unique insight into the genre. The film moves quickly and offers plenty of amusement for country music fans and newcomers alike. Ms. Hobbs has made a promising debut.

(C) The prices were expensive. (D) The food did not taste good.#### Call for Competitors: ANTA TechStar The Australian National Trucking Association (ANTA)'s national TechStar competition recognizes and promotes commercial vehicle maintenance skills. ABOUT THE COMPETITION: The contest will be held on 22–23 September during ANTA's yearly meeting in Brisbane. In the first round, competitors will take a multiple-choice exam and complete basic hands-on tasks related to truck maintenance. The 30 competitors with the highest scores will move on to the second round, where they will be asked to diagnose and correct technical issues in 17 skill areas at timed stations. The winner will be announced at the ANTA Industry Awards Luncheon on 24 September. WHO CAN PARTICIPATE: Winners of regional skills contests held by ANTA's member associations are automatically invited to TechStar, but anyone currently employed as a commercial vehicle technician may register to participate. HOW TO REGISTER: Visit www.anta.org.au/techstar by 25 August to fill out a registration form and pay the registration fee. The fee is waived for regional contest winners and previous TechStar winners. #### ANTA TechStar Winner Announced (25 September)—Simon Freight Services technician Adem Binici was announced as the winner of this year's TechStar competition at yesterday's Australian National Trucking Association (ANTA) Industry Awards Luncheon. According to competition chair Kaitlyn Paul, Mr. Binici took the top score at an extraordinary six out of 17 skill-test stations. None of his competitors, including second-place finisher Leanne Bass, also of Simon Freight Services, managed to receive top marks at more than two. “Our judges were very impressed with Mr. Binici's expertise in a range of vehicle maintenance and repair topics,” Ms. Paul told attendees. “This level of mastery is becoming rare as trucks become more complex.” She cited the fact that this year’s TechStar included testing stations for electric vehicle issues and cybersecurity for the first time. In his acceptance speech, Mr. Binici also mentioned the increasing complexity of maintenance work, and said he plans to continue competing in TechStar to keep his skills sharp. After thanking ANTA, he concluded by expressing his excitement that the contest's prizes always include an expenses-paid trip to attend the Melbourne Motor Racing Championships.181. In addition to the main museum building, our facilities include a fleet of contemporary and historic vessels for exhibition and visitor experiences. We currently have multiple openings for outdoor volunteers. These volunteers are responsible for assisting our staff crews in operating ships for visitor outings on the bay and completing a variety of ship maintenance tasks on our docks. Applicants must be at least 20 years of age and in strong physical shape. Previous experience with sailing and ships is preferred but not necessary. After completing our orientation programme, volunteers will be expected to work at least one three-hour shift each week. To learn more or begin the application process, please contact Troy Russell, our volunteer programme assistant, at trussell@hbmm.org.nz. #### Houghton Bay Maritime Museum Outdoor Volunteer Schedule for Friday, 15 January

Recently, many property holders in the Montgomery Beach area have been listing their properties on online platforms that facilitate short-term rentals. The word “sound” in paragraph 2, line 5, is closest in meaning to (A) critical (B) sensible (C) independent (D) noisy # Treichl Changes Direction on Feature FRANKFURT (18 September)— Treichl, Inc., one of the world's premier vehicle manufacturers, will no longer oblige customers to join a paid subscription service to make use of their car's seat-heating hardware. The company had introduced the practice last year, drawing widespread criticism from automotive enthusiasts. Its newest vehicles, announced this week, either come with fully functional heated seats or do not have them at all. “Our intention behind the offering was to give customers flexibility,” Treichl’s vice president of sales, Beatrix Van Eyck, said when reached for comment. “But people perceived it as having to pay double for a feature, and subscriber numbers were low. We have no plans to maintain or expand subscriptions for factory-installed hardware at present.” However, Ms. Van Eyck noted that the company continues to charge customers monthly for some software features available in their cars, such as parking assistance and DriveLink, its digital service platform with capabilities such as weather and traffic updates. “Customers do seem to appreciate the ability to upgrade, downgrade, or suspend these functions whenever they want, especially when improved versions become available,” said Ms. Van Eyck. 161. In the e-mail, the word “hold” in paragraph 3, line 2, is closest in meaning to (A) support (B) reserve (C) conduct (D) possess### Fashion Unearthed Reviewed by Evelyn Dudley Seiji Otori loves fashion, but he also loves our planet. In his new book, Fashion Unearthed: An Environmentalist's Guide to Looking Good, he explores ways that shopping enthusiasts can indulge their passion for style with minimal harm to the environment. The book includes an impressive catalogue of constructive suggestions, all supported by in-depth research. Overall, Mr. Otori has created a good introduction to issues in the fashion world that focuses on practical options available to consumers. Otori's book is impressive in its scope, as it touches on everything from textile production to retail distribution and recycling practices. He even discusses online reselling platforms, a newer phenomenon that is important because of its popularity and mixed environmental effects. In fact, the only conspicuous omission is that of clothing repair and alteration services, which should be recommended as ways to refresh worn or outdated pieces. This oversight aside, Fashion Unearthed is an informative read with an engagingly optimistic outlook.
E-Mail Message
From:s.otori@mailit.com
To:editor@currentliving.com
Subject:Fashion Unearthed review
Date:May 29
Dear Editor:
It was a thrill to see my book reviewed in your magazine by Evelyn Dudley. I consider her one of the inspirations for Fashion Unearthed, as it was reading her works in college that first got me interested in the issues I discuss in the book. I was pleased that she highlighted the chapter on reselling apps and Web sites, since I also feel that area has not been deeply examined in other publications. She also makes a reasonable point that the range of recommendations could have been wider. In fact, my first draft of the book did include a section on the missing topic she mentioned, but it was cut to streamline the book. Perhaps it could be restored in a future edition.
Seiji Otori

Mr. Freeman joins Brinkley from the high-end Abbottville restaurant L'Artisan, where he was the head pastry chef. Mr. Freeman discovered a passion for making sweet treats by chance at his first job at the Lynn's Bakery chain. He then earned a pastry certification from the Daucourt School of Culinary Arts. His résumé includes positions at illustrious establishments in France and Switzerland, including the five-star Millésime. While at L'Artisan, Mr. Freeman also competed successfully in national pastry-making contests and taught short workshops at Brinkley and other culinary institutions. #### Brinkley Culinary Academy #### Renowned Chefs Series for Fall

To:Michael Huang
From:Jeremy Larsen
Date:June 29
Subject:Paid time off policy
Dear Mr. Huang,
As the compliance specialist for the human resources department, I need to make you aware of a development related to our leave policy. As a reminder, we currently offer our employees between 10 and 20 days of paid time off (PTO) that can be used for any purpose. Unused time rolls over at the end of the calendar year, and upon leaving the company, employees are compensated for up to 10 unused days. However, the regional legislature just passed a regulation requiring companies with more than 15 employees to compensate departing employees for the full value of their unused PTO. This can create a large financial liability for companies in the case of restructuring. We may want to rethink our policies in light of this change.
Sincerely,
Jeremy Larsen
CONSULTING REPORT Rasmussen Insights
July 17 Prepared by: Riley Davis, Senior Consultant Client: Michael Huang, CEO, Wincrest Corporation Executive Summary OBJECTIVE: Determine optimal leave policies for the Wincrest Corporation, based on its operations, data on its employees' use of time off, and industry norms OPTIONS ANALYZED: 1. Continuing all policies in their present form 2. Converting some of the existing PTO allowance into sick time 3. Ending or restricting PTO rollover of 10 days 4. Implementing an “unlimited time off” policy CONCLUSION: Converting some PTO into sick time and restricting PTO rollover are recommended. Care should be taken in the timing and communication of these changes.##### Wincrest Corporation Employee Handbook ##### Leave Policies ##### Section 1 (Revised July 28) A. Upon hiring, employees are entitled to 10 days of PTO and 5 paid sick days per calendar year. The number of PTO days shall increase by 1 day after each complete year of employment up to a maximum of 15. B. No more than 5 days of unused PTO may be rolled over into the following year. Excess days are forfeited without compensation. C. The timing of PTO is subject to a manager's approval. Requests to use PTO must be submitted at least 3 days in advance. D. Sick days may be requested as needed. A doctor's note will be required to use 3 or more days consecutively. 191. What is the purpose of the e-mail? Subject: Ongoing issues with Exis 500 fryer
Date: July 3
Dear Ms. Correa,
On June 8, Telekes installed a pair of new Exis 500 deep fryers at the Southshore branch of Crispy Crab and gave our kitchen staff a short training on them. At first, the machines operated smoothly, and we were pleased with their fast-frying time and oil-saving filtration system.
On June 27, the fryer that we use for non-seafood dishes began to malfunction; it was slow to heat up after oil changes. The Telekes technicians came back on June 29 and fixed this issue, but since then we have found that the temperature sometimes fluctuates. In addition, the changes aren't reflected on the display, so our staff is sometimes not aware of the problem until the food is soggy or burned. We had to throw away no fewer than six batches of French fries last Saturday, July 1, because of this.
We are closed on Mondays, and so I spent this morning trying without success to use the troubleshooting methods explained in the product manual to resolve these issues. I then placed a phone call to the Telekes technicians who visited our restaurant before. They have offered to return on Wednesday but seem unsure about the source of the problems, so I do not want to wait and risk another failed repair attempt. Therefore, I ask that you arrange for the machine to be removed and a new Exis 500 to be installed as soon as possible.
Sincerely,
Futoshi Yasui, Manager\nCrispy Crab, Southshore Branch

$ \underline{\text{HOME}} $ COURSES TOPICS NEWS GAMES JOIN #### CTF Learn French—Your Gateway to the French Language French-speaking countries are popular tourist destinations and active markets for business, but many aspiring French speakers find in-person language classes to be expensive and inconvenient. Fortunately, there is CTF's Learn French Web site. As one of France's premier television and radio broadcast companies, CTF is invested in promoting fluency in the French language. Our Web site offers interactive courses for speakers at every level from absolute beginner to advanced. Actual CTF news videos are used to teach language skills. We even have games like the Grammar Game Show, in which the presenter of a real CTF TV program quizzes learners on their grammar knowledge. Membership costs just €5 per month or €50 for a full year and includes access to all of our content. Members also have the option to receive a daily e-mail with a language tip suited to their fluency level. A discount is available for businesses and nonprofit organizations that purchase 10 or more memberships. Click the "Join" link above to sign up and get started today. https://www.ctf-learnfrench.fr/userreviews
#### Reviews for CTF's Learn French Web site ##### ★★★★☆ I work for a construction supplier that is attempting to expand into French-speaking African countries, so I took advantage of Learn French's organizational discount to purchase memberships for my team. Comprehensive analyses of the collected data have been delayed by technical problems. Once they are completed, I will edit the report to reflect the additional findings. Helplines: The overall satisfaction level with this support channel has reached a record high among our domestic customers. The average ratings from Canadian customers have recovered after falling for the past two years. In the United Kingdom and the United States, satisfaction levels remain lower than ideal. User Forums: Satisfaction levels continued to be solid here in Ireland this year, while among international customers they varied but improved slightly in all markets. Live Chat: Likely due to bugs in the new live-chat platform we have been testing here, there was a sharp drop in domestic customer satisfaction. Fortunately, the figures moved in the opposite direction in Canada and remained flat but decent in other countries. Documentation: Local Irish customers have stayed fairly well satisfied with our offerings here, while the upward trend among Canadians continues. In the United States and United Kingdom, there were minor declines, returning numbers to the decent levels they were at before last year's surprising upticks.
To:hhwang@lyonsoft.ie
From:bfrazier@lyonsoft.ie
Date:22 November
Subject:Satisfaction survey
Dear Hyo-Rin,\nI appreciated your sending over the report on the customer service survey. It was disappointing to see that opinions on the support we provide through Flexision are still quite negative even though we have resolved the glitches that the software had when the trial period began. In perhaps six months or so, when domestic customers have seen that it has improved, then we can proceed with our plans. How does that sound?
Bruce Frazier

I have some strong opinions! —Omar # Around Calgary June 30 The five-year project to demolish Howard Events Centre and replace it with a larger complex begins today. The venue closed its doors to the public for the final time last weekend following a sold-out performance by Sonic Spectrum. City Councillor Stella Gardner says the renovated complex will raise the city's status by attracting major events and asks residents to be patient with the inconveniences of construction. Nearby businesses that relied on Howard events to generate foot traffic are seeking ways to continue attracting visitors to the area. Whitney Gordon, owner of Sloane Inn, said she is trying several approaches to the problem. Work is already underway to build an 800-square-metre ballroom that will double the inn's event capacity. She and co-owner Lucas Frye are also hoping to collaborate with other downtown hotels and conference venues to host large events jointly.##### E-Mail Message

Kylie Donovan Volunteer Program Director, Whitaker Community Center
To create your Valiant account, go to https://valiantvolunteer.com/register-volunteer and follow the steps below. 1. Provide the requested personal information and choose a password. Enter the same e-mail address as the one you provided to our community center. Click "Continue." 2. You will be shown Valiant's Terms and Conditions page. Confirm your agreement. 3. A page titled "E-mail Verification" will appear. Permit Valiant to send you a verification e-mail. 4. Go to your e-mail inbox, open the message, and click the provided link to complete the verification process. Once your e-mail has been verified, you may sign in to your Valiant account and begin booking shifts.
